# VA human review — proposed class and case decisions

7 class items; 83 case items. All are unsigned proposals. No sealed pass or adjudication was changed.

Class decisions settle only the named policy facet; concrete targets, dates and missing evidence remain cases. Issue padding maps verified hash preimages and is an identity candidate, not a disagreement. Latest append-only label-chain heads are compared once. Reference wording is retained in pairs.wording_diffs.

## Counts by trigger

| Trigger | Class | Case | Minutes |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| disagreement | 1 | 22 | 92 |
| lifecycle_ambiguity | 1 | 16 | 72 |
| date_ambiguity | 2 | 7 | 43 |
| visual_mark | 0 | 4 | 20 |
| missing_source | 0 | 9 | 37 |
| identity | 1 | 0 | 10 |
| scope | 2 | 25 | 111 |

`va-policy-b13a3486bbc7f27980d96c267b1154a3cc0f58f102836161223911f37d8d59d9`; 168 instances; 335 label versions; 10 min.

**Proposed:** Keep supported opening wording or an explicit unknown/null opening. Same as CT C15 (pending owner signature).

The brief requires applicable open roles; publication metadata supplies no legal opening instant.

Owner may accept, reject or specify exceptions by immutable record ID; nothing is applied.

### C02. Confirm fallback identities supported by the same original region?

`va-policy-447255236e53764a4a7b58d55f1a3cf449d2760b5d90302029f69b9d448b635d`; 82 instances; 164 label versions; 10 min.

**Proposed:** One entity for each enumerated one-to-one original-region/chapter/role match; preserve both sealed IDs in a crosswalk.

Fallback locators differ between passes. Region plus chapter or linked participation values supports identity; no match uses title alone.

Owner may accept, reject or specify exceptions by immutable record ID; nothing is applied.

### C03. Which issue date controls 42:25 interval accounting?

`va-policy-cdebf9126f405397fb0666e33ecaaaf598ad0aa597a9749fba3ef36e7006f324`; 59 instances; 116 label versions; 10 min.

**Proposed:** Propose July 27, 2026 issue date; remove unrelated new notices from the scored interval and retain linked follow-up only within its horizon.

Cover July 25 conflicts with body footers and other supplied official issue views. Boundary uncertainty cannot be resolved by agent agreement.

Owner may accept, reject or specify exceptions by immutable record ID; nothing is applied.

### C07. Reconcile completion counts with final source-view attribution?

`va-policy-ea8e131f9df1219f64e126974438059b9527b59add884d621cd07250a07de74e`; 18 instances; 230 label versions; 10 min.

**Proposed:** Final source-view attribution, corrected append-only; same as CT C16 (pending owner signature). Verify the 18 B discrepancies, including two intentionally counted duplicate views; never sum views or equate absent evidence links with an uninspected source.

Final historical labels can postdate completion rows. Direct-evidence counts are a diagnostic; the brief permits overlapping source-view attribution. A TOC counts also require source-attribution interpretation, not automatic zeroing.

Owner may accept, reject or specify exceptions by immutable record ID; nothing is applied.
